[]).map(normalizePath));\n107\t  const files = roots\n108\t    .map((root) => resolveInsideWorkspace(workspaceRoot, root))\n109\t    .filter((root) => workspacePathExists(root))\n110\t    .flatMap((root) => collectSourceAndTestFiles(root));\n111\t  const snapshotFiles = filterFilesBySnapshot(files, workspaceRoot, input);\n112\t  const units = snapshotFiles.map((file) => readFileUnit(file, workspaceRoot));\n113\t  const tests = units.filter((unit) => unit.isTest);\n114\t  const sources = units.filter((unit) => !unit.isTest);\n115\t  const sourcePaths = new Set(sources.map((source) => source.relativePath));\n116\t  const pathAliases = readPathAliases(workspaceRoot);\n117\t  const result: AnalysisResult = {\n118\t    observations: [],\n119\t    findings: [],\n120\t    readPaths: units.map((unit) => unit.relativePath),\n121\t  };\n122\t\n123\t  for (const test of tests) {\n124\t    result.observations.push({\n125\t      id: `test-gap-test-file:${test.relativePath}`,\n126\t      type: 'test_gap_test_file',\n127\t      path: test.relativePath,\n128\t      line: 1,\n129\t      name: basename(test.relativePath),\n130\t      details: {\n131\t        imports: importedSpecifiers(test.sourceFile).sort(),\n132\t      },\n133\t    });\n134\t  }\n135\t\n136\t  let highRiskWithoutTest = 0;\n137\t  let directMatchCount = 0;\n138\t  let weakMatchCount = 0;\n139\t  for (const source of sources) {\n140\t    const risk = classifySourceRisk(source);\n141\t    if (!risk.highRisk && !risk.securitySensitive && !risk.migrationHazard) {\n142\t      continue;\n143\t    }\n144\t    const matchedTests = matchingTests(source, tests, sourcePaths, pathAliases);\n145\t    const weakMatchedTests = weakSymbolTests(source, tests).filter(\n146\t      (test) => !matchedTests.some((matched) => matched.relativePath === test.relativePath),\n147\t    );\n148\t    directMatchCount += matchedTests.length > 0 ? /(\\.controller|\\.resolver|\\.handler|\\.guard|\\.strategy|\\.middleware|\\.interceptor)\\.ts$/.test(path);\n271\t  const securitySensitive = /dangerouslySetInnerHTML|DOMPurify|sanitizeHtml|@Public\\b|AuthGuard|TenantGuard|PermissionGuard/i.test(text)\n272\t    || /(\\.guard|\\.strategy|\\/auth\\/|\\/security\\/|\\/permission)/i.test(path);\n273\t  const writeBoundary = /@(Post|Put|Patch|Delete|Mutation)\\b|\\b(create|update|delete|remove|archive|approve|reject|reset|invite)\\w*\\s*\\(/i.test(text);\n274\t  const migrationHazard = /\\/migrations\\/.*\\.ts$/.test(path)\n275\t    && /\\b(DROP|DELETE|TRUNCATE|ALTER\\s+TYPE|ENABLE\\s+ROW\\s+LEVEL\\s+SECURITY|FORCE\\s+ROW\\s+LEVEL\\s+SECURITY)\\b/i.test(text);\n276\t  return { riskClass, highRisk, securitySensitive, migrationHazard, writeBoundary };\n277\t}\n278\t\n279\tfunction riskClassForPath(path: string): string {\n280\t  if (path.includes('/migrations/')) {\n281\t    return 'migration';\n282\t  }\n283\t  // match[1] is narrowed explicitly so the file stays clean under\n284\t  // --noUncheckedIndexedAccess (the capture group is structurally guaranteed,\n285\t  // but the index signature cannot prove it to the compiler).\n286\t  const match = path.match(/\\.([a-z-]+)\\.(ts|tsx)$/);\n287\t  if (match?.[1] !== undefined) {\n288\t    return match[1];\n289\t  }\n290\t  if (path.endsWith('.tsx')) {\n291\t    return 'ui_component';\n292\t  }\n293\t  return 'source';\n294\t}\n295\t\n296\tfunction matchingTests(\n297\t  source: FileUnit,\n298\t  tests: basenameWithoutKnownSuffix(source.relativePath);\n303\t  const sourceDir = dirname(source.relativePath);\n304\t  return tests.filter((test) => {\n305\t    const testBase = basenameWithoutKnownSuffix(test.relativePath);\n306\t    if (testBase === sourceBase) {\n307\t      return true;\n308\t    }\n309\t    if (dirname(test.relativePath) === sourceDir && testBase.startsWith(sourceBase)) {\n310\t      return true;\n311\t    }\n312\t    if (dirname(test.relativePath).endsWith(`${sourceDir}/__tests__`) && testBase.includes(sourceBase)) {\n313\t      return true;\n314\t    }\n315\t    return importedSpecifiers(test.sourceFile).some((specifier) => {\n316\t      const resolved = resolveImportSpecifier(test.relativePath, specifier, sourcePaths, .replace(/\\.(controller|resolver|handler|guard|strategy|middleware|interceptor|service|entity|module|component)\\.(ts|tsx)$/, '')\n334\t    .replace(/\\.(ts|tsx)$/, '');\n335\t}\n336\t\n337\tfunction resolveImportSpecifier(\n348\t  importingPath: string,\n349\t  specifier: string,\n350\t  sourcePaths: ReadonlySet<string>,\n351\t  pathAliases: readonly PathAlias[],\n352\t): string | undefined {\n353\t  const candidates: string[] = [];\n354\t  if (specifier.startsWith('.')) {\n355\t    candidates.push(normalizePath(join(dirname(importingPath), specifier)));\n356\t  } else {\n357\t    for (const alias of pathAliases) {\n358\t      if (!specifier.startsWith(alias.prefix) || !specifier.endsWith(alias.suffix)) {\n359\t        continue;\n360\t      }\n361\t      const middle = specifier.slice(alias.prefix.length, specifier.length - alias.suffix.length);\n362\t      for (const target of alias.targets) {\n363\t        candidates.push(target.replace('*', middle));\n364\t      }\n365\t    }\n366\t  }\n367\t  for (const candidate of candidates.flatMap(candidatePathVariants)) {\n368\t    if (sourcePaths.has(candidate)) {\n369\t      return candidate;\n370\t    }\n371\t  }\n372\t  return undefined;\n373\t}\n374\t\n375\tfunction candidatePathVariants(candidate: string): string[] {\n376\t  const normalized = normalizePath(candidate).replace(/^\\.\\//, '');\n377\t  return [\n378\t    normalized,\n379\t    `${normalized}.ts`,\n380\t    `${normalized}.tsx`,\n381\t    `${normalized}/index.ts`,\n382\t    `${normalized}/index.tsx`,\n383\t  ];\n384\t}\n385\t\n386\tfunction readPathAliases(workspaceRoot: string): readonly PathAlias[] {\n387\t  const path = resolve(workspaceRoot, 'tsconfig.base.json');\n388\t  if (!workspacePathExists(path)) {\n389\t    return [];\n390\t  }\n391\t  try {\n392\t    const parsed = JSON.parse(readWorkspaceFile(path)) as {\n393\t      compilerOptions?: { paths?: Record<string, readonly string[]> };\n394\t    };\n395\t    return Object.entries(parsed.compilerOptions?.paths ?? {}).map(([key, targets]) => {\n396\t      const starIndex = key.indexOf('*');\n397\t      return {\n398\t        prefix: starIndex >= 0 ? key.slice(0, starIndex) : key,\n399\t        suffix: starIndex >= 0 ? key.slice(starIndex + 1) : '',\n400\t        targets: targets.map((target) => normalizePath(target)),\n401\t      };\n402\t    });\n403\t  } catch {\n404\t    return [];\n405\t  }\n406\t}\n407\t\n408\tfunction exportedSymbols(sourceFile: ts.SourceFile): string[] {\n409\t  const symbols = new Set<string>();\n410\t  visit(sourceFile, (node) => {\n411\t    if ((ts.isClassDeclaration(node) || ts.isFunctionDeclaration(node) || ts.isInterfaceDeclaration(node) || ts.isTypeAliasDeclaration(node)) && node.name) {\n412\t      const modifiers = ts.canHaveModifiers(node) ? ts.getModifiers(node) ?? [] : [];\n413\t      if (modifiers.some((modifier) => modifier.kind === ts.SyntaxKind.ExportKeyword)) {\n414\t        symbols.add(node.name.text);\n415\t      }\n416\t    }\n417\t  });\n418\t  return Array.from(symbols).sort();\n419\t}\n420\t\n421\tfunction readFileUnit(path: string, workspaceRoot: string): FileUnit {\n422\t  const text = readWorkspaceFile(path);\n423\t  return {\n424\t    path,\n425\t    relativePath: normalizePath(relative(workspaceRoot, path)),\n426\t    text,\n427\t    sourceFile: ts.createSourceFile(path, text, ts.ScriptTarget.Latest, true),\n428\t    isTest: spec must not satisfy a live source), so it is excluded from\n438\t    // the scan entirely rather than special-cased at finding time.\n439\t    includeFile: (name, path) => !name.endsWith('.d.ts') && !isArchivedWorkspacePath(path),\n440\t  });\n441\t}\n442\t\n443\tfunction isTestFile(path: string): boolean {\n444\t  const normalized = normalizePath(path);\n445\t  return resolveAdapterPath(workspaceRoot, requestedPath);\n450\t}\n451\t\n452\tfunction visit(node: ts.Node, visitor: (node: ts.Node) => void): void {\n453\t  visitor(node);\n454\t  ts.forEachChild(node, a.id.localeCompare(b.id);\n459\t}\n460\t\n461\tfunction normalizePath(path: string): string {\n462\t  return normalizeWorkspacePath(path);\n463\t}\n464\t\n465\tfunction readStdin(): Promise<string> main().catch((error: unknown) => {\n489\t    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);\n490\t    process.stderr.write(`${message}\\n`);\n491\t    process.exit(1);\n492\t ts-node\nimport { basename, dirname, join, relative, resolve } from 'node:path';\n\nimport ts from 'typescript';\n\nimport {\n  collectFiles,\n  filterFilesBySnapshot,\n  isArchivedWorkspacePath,\n  normalizeWorkspacePath,\n  readWorkspaceFile,\n  requireScanRoots,\n  resolveInsideWorkspace as resolveAdapterPath,\n  workspacePathExists,\n} from './adapter-fs';\n\ntype FindingRule =\n  | 'high_risk_source_without_adjacent_test'\n  | 'migration_without_test'\n  | 'security_source_without_security_test';\n\ninterface AdapterInput {\n  readonly roots?: readonly string[];\n  readonly allowlist?: readonly string[];\n  readonly includeWriteBoundaryFindings?: boolean;\n  readonly repo_snapshot?: { readonly allowed_paths?: readonly string[]; readonly snapshot_hash?: string; readonly repo_state_id?: string };\n}\n\ninterface EvidenceRef {\n  readonly path: string;\n  readonly line?: number;\n}\n\ninterface AdapterObservation {\n  readonly id: string;\n  readonly type: string;\n  readonly path?: string;\n  readonly line?: number;\n  readonly name?: string;\n  readonly details?: Record<string, unknown>;\n}\n\ninterface AdapterFinding {\n  readonly id: string;\n  readonly rule: FindingRule;\n  readonly severity: 'medium' | 'high';\n  readonly path: string;\n  readonly line?: number;\n  readonly message: string;\n  readonly evidence: readonly EvidenceRef[];\n  readonly confidence?: number;\n  readonly actionability?: 'actionable' | 'review_required';\n  readonly review_reason?: string;\n  readonly details?: Record<string, unknown>;\n}\n\ninterface MemoryCandidate {\n  readonly belief_id: string;\n  readonly claim: string;\n  readonly confidence: number;\n  readonly evidence_refs: readonly string[];\n  readonly source_tool_id: string;\n}\n\ninterface AriaOutput {\n  readonly observations: readonly AdapterObservation[];\n  readonly findings: readonly AdapterFinding[];\n  readonly read_paths: readonly string[];\n  readonly evidence_sources: readonly string[];\n  readonly belief_candidates: readonly MemoryCandidate[];\n  readonly cost_units: number;\n  readonly metadata: Record<string, unknown>;\n}\n\ninterface FileUnit {\n  readonly path: string;\n  readonly relativePath: string;\n  readonly text: string;\n  readonly sourceFile: ts.SourceFile;\n  readonly isTest: boolean;\n}\n\ninterface SourceRisk {\n  readonly riskClass: string;\n  readonly highRisk: boolean;\n  readonly securitySensitive: boolean;\n  readonly migrationHazard: boolean;\n  readonly writeBoundary: boolean;\n}\n\ninterface AnalysisResult {\n  readonly observations: AdapterObservation[];\n  readonly findings: AdapterFinding[];\n  readonly readPaths: string[];\n}\n\ninterface PathAlias {\n  readonly prefix: string;\n  readonly suffix: string;\n  readonly targets: readonly string[];\n}\n\n\nexport function analyzeTestGaps(input: AdapterInput, workspaceRoot = process.cwd()): AriaOutput {\n  const roots = requireScanRoots('test-gap-adapter', input.roots);\n  const allowlist = new Set((input.allowlist ?? []).map(normalizePath));\n  const files = roots\n    .map((root) => resolveInsideWorkspace(workspaceRoot, root))\n    .filter((root) => workspacePathExists(root))\n    .flatMap((root) => collectSourceAndTestFiles(root));\n  const snapshotFiles = filterFilesBySnapshot(files, workspaceRoot, input);\n  const units = snapshotFiles.map((file) => readFileUnit(file, workspaceRoot));\n  const tests = units.filter((unit) => unit.isTest);\n  const sources = units.filter((unit) => !unit.isTest);\n  const sourcePaths = new Set(sources.map((source) => source.relativePath));\n  const pathAliases = readPathAliases(workspaceRoot);\n  const result: AnalysisResult = {\n    observations: [],\n    findings: [],\n    readPaths: units.map((unit) => unit.relativePath),\n  };\n\n  for (const test of tests) {\n    result.observations.push({\n      id: `test-gap-test-file:${test.relativePath}`,\n      type: 'test_gap_test_file',\n      path: test.relativePath,\n      line: 1,\n      name: basename(test.relativePath),\n      details: {\n        imports: importedSpecifiers(test.sourceFile).sort(),\n      },\n    });\n  }\n\n  let highRiskWithoutTest = 0;\n  let directMatchCount = 0;\n  let weakMatchCount = 0;\n  for (const source of sources) {\n    const risk = classifySourceRisk(source);\n    if (!risk.highRisk && !risk.securitySensitive && !risk.migrationHazard) {\n      continue;\n    }\n    const matchedTests = matchingTests(source, tests, sourcePaths, pathAliases);\n    const weakMatchedTests = weakSymbolTests(source, tests).filter(\n      (test) => !matchedTests.some((matched) => matched.relativePath === test.relativePath),\n    );\n    directMatchCount += matchedTests.length > 0 ? 1 : 0;\n    weakMatchCount += weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 1 : 0;\n    const allowlisted = allowlist.has(source.relativePath);\n    result.observations.push({\n      id: `test-gap-source-file:${source.relativePath}`,\n      type: 'test_gap_source_file',\n      path: source.relativePath,\n      line: 1,\n      name: basename(source.relativePath),\n      details: {\n        riskClass: risk.riskClass,\n        highRisk: risk.highRisk,\n        securitySensitive: risk.securitySensitive,\n        migrationHazard: risk.migrationHazard,\n        writeBoundary: risk.writeBoundary,\n        matchedTests: matchedTests.map((test) => test.relativePath).sort(),\n        weakMatchedTests: weakMatchedTests.map((test) => test.relativePath).sort(),\n        allowlisted,\n      },\n    });\n    if (allowlisted || matchedTests.length > 0) {\n      continue;\n    }\n    if (risk.migrationHazard) {\n      highRiskWithoutTest += 1;\n      result.findings.push({\n        id: `migration-without-test:${source.relativePath}`,\n        rule: 'migration_without_test',\n        severity: 'high',\n        path: source.relativePath,\n        message: 'Hazardous migration has no adjacent or importing test coverage signal.',\n        evidence: [{ path: source.relativePath }],\n        confidence: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 0.55 : 0.88,\n        actionability: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 'review_required' : 'actionable',\n        review_reason: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 'weak_symbol_test_match' : undefined,\n        details: { riskClass: risk.riskClass, weakMatchedTests: weakMatchedTests.map((test) => test.relativePath).sort() },\n      });\n      continue;\n    }\n    if (risk.securitySensitive) {\n      highRiskWithoutTest += 1;\n      result.findings.push({\n        id: `security-source-without-test:${source.relativePath}`,\n        rule: 'security_source_without_security_test',\n        severity: 'high',\n        path: source.relativePath,\n        message: 'Security-sensitive source file has no adjacent or importing test coverage signal.',\n        evidence: [{ path: source.relativePath }],\n        confidence: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 0.55 : 0.86,\n        actionability: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 'review_required' : 'actionable',\n        review_reason: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 'weak_symbol_test_match' : undefined,\n        details: { riskClass: risk.riskClass, weakMatchedTests: weakMatchedTests.map((test) => test.relativePath).sort() },\n      });\n      continue;\n    }\n    if (input.includeWriteBoundaryFindings === true && risk.highRisk && risk.writeBoundary) {\n      highRiskWithoutTest += 1;\n      result.findings.push({\n        id: `high-risk-source-without-adjacent-test:${source.relativePath}`,\n        rule: 'high_risk_source_without_adjacent_test',\n        severity: 'medium',\n        path: source.relativePath,\n        message: 'High-risk source file has no adjacent, sibling __tests__, or importing test coverage signal.',\n        evidence: [{ path: source.relativePath }],\n        confidence: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 0.5 : 0.75,\n        actionability: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 'review_required' : 'actionable',\n        review_reason: weakMatchedTests.length > 0 ? 'weak_symbol_test_match' : undefined,\n        details: { riskClass: risk.riskClass, weakMatchedTests: weakMatchedTests.map((test) => test.relativePath).sort() },\n      });\n    }\n  }\n\n  result.observations.push({\n    id: 'test-gap-coverage-summary',\n    type: 'test_gap_coverage_summary',\n    details: {\n      sourceFiles: sources.length,\n      testFiles: tests.length,\n      directMatchCount,\n      weakMatchCount,\n      highRiskWithoutTest,\n      unmatchedHighRiskCount: highRiskWithoutTest,\n      scanMode: 'import_graph_v1',\n    },\n  });\n\n  result.observations.sort(compareById);\n  result.findings.sort(compareById);\n  result.readPaths.sort();\n  const evidenceSources = Array.from(\n    new Set(result.findings.flatMap((finding) => finding.evidence.map((evidence) => evidence.path))),\n  ).sort();\n\n  return {\n    observations: result.observations,\n    findings: result.findings,\n    read_paths: Array.from(new Set(result.readPaths)).sort(),\n    evidence_sources: evidenceSources,\n    belief_candidates: [\n      {\n        belief_id: 'test-gap:high-risk-source-coverage-surface',\n        claim: 'high-risk 'platform/libs/**/*.ts', 'web/**/*.tsx'],\n        source_tool_id: 'test-gap-adapter',\n      },\n    ],\n    cost_units: Array.from(new Set(result.readPaths)).length,\n    metadata: {\n      adapter:  };\n}\n\nfunction classifySourceRisk(unit: FileUnit): SourceRisk {\n  const path = unit.relativePath;\n  const text = unit.text;\n  const riskClass = riskClassForPath(path);\n  const highRisk = /(\\.controller|\\.resolver|\\.handler|\\.guard|\\.strategy|\\.middleware|\\.interceptor)\\.ts$/.test(path);\n  const securitySensitive = /dangerouslySetInnerHTML|DOMPurify|sanitizeHtml|@Public\\b|AuthGuard|TenantGuard|PermissionGuard/i.test(text)\n    || /(\\.guard|\\.strategy|\\/auth\\/|\\/security\\/|\\/permission)/i.test(path);\n  const writeBoundary = /@(Post|Put|Patch|Delete|Mutation)\\b|\\b(create|update|delete|remove|archive|approve|reject|reset|invite)\\w*\\s*\\(/i.test(text);\n  const migrationHazard = /\\/migrations\\/.*\\.ts$/.test(path)\n    && /\\b(DROP|DELETE|TRUNCATE|ALTER\\s+TYPE|ENABLE\\s+ROW\\s+LEVEL\\s+SECURITY|FORCE\\s+ROW\\s+LEVEL\\s+SECURITY)\\b/i.test(text);\n  return { riskClass, highRisk, securitySensitive, migrationHazard, writeBoundary };\n}\n\nfunction riskClassForPath(path: string): string {\n  if (path.includes('/migrations/')) {\n    return 'migration';\n  }\n  // match[1] is narrowed explicitly so the file stays clean under\n  // --noUncheckedIndexedAccess (the capture group is structurally guaranteed,\n  // but the index signature cannot prove it to the compiler).\n  const match = path.match(/\\.([a-z-]+)\\.(ts|tsx)$/);\n  if (match?.[1] !== undefined) {\n    return match[1];\n  }\n  if (path.endsWith('.tsx')) {\n    return 'ui_component';\n  }\n  return 'source';\n}\n\nfunction matchingTests(\n  source: FileUnit,\n  tests: readonly FileUnit[],\n  sourcePaths: ReadonlySet<string>,\n  pathAliases: readonly PathAlias[],\n): readonly FileUnit[] {\n  const sourceBase = basenameWithoutKnownSuffix(source.relativePath);\n  const sourceDir = dirname(source.relativePath);\n  return tests.filter((test) => {\n    const testBase = basenameWithoutKnownSuffix(test.relativePath);\n    if (testBase === sourceBase) {\n      return true;\n    }\n    if (dirname(test.relativePath) === sourceDir && testBase.startsWith(sourceBase)) {\n      return true;\n    }\n    if (dirname(test.relativePath).endsWith(`${sourceDir}/__tests__`) && testBase.includes(sourceBase)) {\n      return true;\n    }\n    return importedSpecifiers(test.sourceFile).some((specifier) => {\n      const resolved = resolveImportSpecifier(test.relativePath, specifier, sourcePaths, pathAliases);\n      return resolved === source.relativePath;\n    });\n  });\n}\n\nfunction weakSymbolTests(source: FileUnit, tests: readonly FileUnit[]): readonly FileUnit[] {\n  const symbolNames = exportedSymbols(source.sourceFile);\n  if (symbolNames.length === 0) {\n    return [];\n  }\n  return tests.filter((test) => symbolNames.some((symbolName) => symbolName.length > 2 && test.text.includes(symbolName)));\n}\n\nfunction basenameWithoutKnownSuffix(path: string): string {\n  return basename(path)\n    .replace(/\\.(spec|test)\\.(ts|tsx)$/, '')\n    .replace(/\\.(controller|resolver|handler|guard|strategy|middleware|interceptor|service|entity|module|component)\\.(ts|tsx)$/, '')\n    .replace(/\\.(ts|tsx)$/, '');\n}\n\nfunction importedSpecifiers(sourceFile: ts.SourceFile): string[] {\n  const imports: string[] = [];\n  for (const statement of sourceFile.statements) {\n    if (ts.isImportDeclaration(statement) && ts.isStringLiteral(statement.moduleSpecifier)) {\n      imports.push(statement.moduleSpecifier.text);\n    }\n  }\n  return imports;\n}\n\nfunction resolveImportSpecifier(\n  importingPath: string,\n  specifier: string,\n  sourcePaths: ReadonlySet<string>,\n  pathAliases: readonly PathAlias[],\n): string | undefined {\n  const candidates: string[] = [];\n  if (specifier.startsWith('.')) {\n    candidates.push(normalizePath(join(dirname(importingPath), specifier)));\n  } else {\n    for (const alias of pathAliases) {\n      if (!specifier.startsWith(alias.prefix) || !specifier.endsWith(alias.suffix)) {\n        continue;\n      }\n      const middle = specifier.slice(alias.prefix.length, specifier.length - alias.suffix.length);\n      for (const target of alias.targets) {\n        candidates.push(target.replace('*', middle));\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  for (const candidate of candidates.flatMap(candidatePathVariants)) {\n    if (sourcePaths.has(candidate)) {\n      return candidate;\n    }\n  }\n  return undefined;\n}\n\nfunction candidatePathVariants(candidate: string): string[] {\n  const normalized = normalizePath(candidate).replace(/^\\.\\//, '');\n  return [\n    normalized,\n    `${normalized}.ts`,\n    `${normalized}.tsx`,\n    `${normalized}/index.ts`,\n    `${normalized}/index.tsx`,\n  ];\n}\n\nfunction readPathAliases(workspaceRoot: string): readonly PathAlias[] {\n  const path = resolve(workspaceRoot, 'tsconfig.base.json');\n  if (!workspacePathExists(path)) {\n    return [];\n  }\n  try {\n    const parsed = JSON.parse(readWorkspaceFile(path)) as {\n      compilerOptions?: { paths?: Record<string, readonly string[]> };\n    };\n    return Object.entries(parsed.compilerOptions?.paths ?? {}).map(([key, targets]) => {\n      const starIndex = key.indexOf('*');\n      return {\n        prefix: starIndex >= 0 ? key.slice(0, starIndex) : key,\n        suffix: starIndex >= 0 ? key.slice(starIndex + 1) : '',\n        targets: targets.map((target) => normalizePath(target)),\n      };\n    });\n  } catch {\n    return [];\n  }\n}\n\nfunction exportedSymbols(sourceFile: ts.SourceFile): string[] {\n  const symbols = new Set<string>();\n  visit(sourceFile, (node) => {\n    if ((ts.isClassDeclaration(node) || ts.isFunctionDeclaration(node) || ts.isInterfaceDeclaration(node) || ts.isTypeAliasDeclaration(node)) && node.name) {\n      const modifiers = ts.canHaveModifiers(node) ? ts.getModifiers(node) ?? [] : [];\n      if (modifiers.some((modifier) => modifier.kind === ts.SyntaxKind.ExportKeyword)) {\n        symbols.add(node.name.text);\n      }\n    }\n  });\n  return Array.from(symbols).sort();\n}\n\nfunction readFileUnit(path: string, workspaceRoot: string): FileUnit {\n  const text = readWorkspaceFile(path);\n  return {\n    path,\n    relativePath: normalizePath(relative(workspaceRoot, path)),\n    text,\n    sourceFile: ts.createSourceFile(path, text, ts.ScriptTarget.Latest, true),\n    isTest: isTestFile(path),\n  };\n}\n\nfunction collectSourceAndTestFiles(root: string): readonly string[] {\n  return collectFiles(root, {\n    extensions: ['.ts', '.tsx'],\n    // E13 FP class (2): archived corpus (e.g. migrations/.archive/<timestamp>/)\n    // is dead code — it can neither need test coverage nor PROVIDE it (an\n    // archived spec must not satisfy a live source), so it is excluded from\n    // the scan entirely rather than special-cased at finding time.\n    includeFile: (name, path) => !name.endsWith('.d.ts') && !isArchivedWorkspacePath(path),\n  });\n}\n\nfunction isTestFile(path: string): boolean {\n  const normalized = normalizePath(path);\n  return /(__tests__|\\.spec\\.|\\.test\\.)/.test(normalized);\n}\n\nfunction resolveInsideWorkspace(workspaceRoot: string, requestedPath: string): string {\n  return resolveAdapterPath(workspaceRoot, requestedPath);\n}\n\nfunction visit(node: ts.Node, visitor: (node: ts.Node) => void): void {\n  visitor(node);\n  ts.forEachChild(node, (child) => visit(child, visitor));\n}\n\nfunction compareById(a: { readonly id: string }, b: { readonly id: string }): number {\n  return a.id.localeCompare(b.id);\n}\n\nfunction normalizePath(path: string): string {\n  return normalizeWorkspacePath(path);\n}\n\nfunction readStdin(): Promise<string> {\n  return new Promise((resolvePromise, reject) => {\n    let input = '';\n    process.stdin.setEncoding('utf8');\n    // setEncoding('utf8') makes every chunk a string at runtime, but the\n    // stream's declared chunk type stays `string | Buffer` — concatenating the\n    // union is what the type checker rejects. Narrow at the boundary rather\n    // than widening `input` (kernel-dead-wire-adapter is the converged shape).\n    process.stdin.on('data', (chunk: string | Buffer) => {\n      input += typeof chunk === 'string' ? chunk : chunk.toString('utf8');\n    });\n    process.stdin.on('end', () => resolvePromise(input));\n    process.stdin.on('error', reject);\n  });\n}\n\nasync function main(): Promise<void> {\n  const rawInput = await readStdin();\n  const input = rawInput.trim().length > 0 ? (JSON.parse(rawInput) as AdapterInput) : {};\n  process.stdout.write(`${JSON.stringify(analyzeTestGaps(input))}\\n`);\n}\n\nif (require.main === module) {\n  main().catch((error: unknown) => {\n    const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);\n    process.stderr.write(`${message}\\n`);\n    process.exit(1);\n  });\n}\n","numLines":494,"startLine":1,"totalLines":494}}}

the deploy 4 DROP TYPE cascade scenario', () => {\n183\t      const sql = `DROP TYPE \"hr\".\"work_week_day\"`;\n184\t      expect(isValidatorRelevant(sql)).toBe(false);\n185\t    });\n186\t  });\n187\t});\n188\t"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"9842d2e2-2674-4c40-84ec-bcd1c4474f20","uuid":"c19c974b-86fc-4183-b378-b1f392f3a833","timestamp":"2026-09-19T16:25:41.454Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-a29dcc8b0754/apps/hr-service/src/database/__tests__/sync-hr-entities-to-db.spec.ts","content":"/**\n * Regex unit tests for SyncHrEntitiesToDb1786800000000's whitelist + idempotency.\n *\n * The migration's two helper predicates (`isValidatorRelevant` and\n * `makeIdempotent`) are inlined inside its `up()` method to keep the\n * migration self-contained. We re-declare the SAME regex/replacement\n * shapes here and assert each known log() output statement class\n * receives the expected verdict.\n *\n * If the migration's inline helpers are ever edited, these tests must\n * be updated to match — both copies are mirrored 1:1 by intent.\n *\n * Failure modes this test catches:\n *   - Whitelist accidentally allows ADD CONSTRAINT (deploy 5 regression)\n *   - Whitelist accidentally rejects ALTER COLUMN TYPE (uuid drift goes uncaught)\n *   - makeIdempotent fails to inject IF NOT EXISTS into ADD COLUMN\n *   - makeIdempotent corrupts schema-qualified table names\n */\n\n// `export {}` keeps strict-tsc treating this file as a MODULE so its\n// top-level declarations stay file-scoped (PROC-MEDIUM-010 invariant).\nexport {};\ndescribe('SyncHrEntitiesToDb1786800000000 — Phase L whitelist + idempotency', () => {\n  // Mirrors apps/hr-service/src/database/migrations/1786800000000-SyncHrEntitiesToDb.ts\n  // §6 isValidatorRelevant. Update BOTH if either changes.\n  const isValidatorRelevant = (sql: string): boolean => {\n    const t = sql.trim();\n    if (/^CREATE\\s+TYPE\\b/i.test(t)) return true;\n    if (/^CREATE\\s+TABLE\\b/i.test(t)) return true;\n    if (/^ALTER\\s+TABLE\\b[^;]*?\\bADD\\s+(?!CONSTRAINT\\b)\"/i.test(t)) return true;\n    // Phase L+: any ALTER COLUMN sub-action passes (TYPE / SET NOT NULL\n    // / DROP NOT NULL / SET DEFAULT / DROP DEFAULT). The trio\n    // DROP-DEFAULT → TYPE → SET-DEFAULT must stay together for PG's\n    // enum type changes to succeed (deploy 6 regression).\n    if (/^ALTER\\s+TABLE\\b[^;]*?\\bALTER\\s+COLUMN\\b/i.test(t)) return true;\n    return false;\n  };\n\n  // Mirrors §6b makeIdempotent. Update BOTH if either changes.\n  const makeIdempotent = (sql: string): string => {\n    let s = sql;\n    s = s.replace(/^CREATE\\s+TABLE\\s+\"/i, 'CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS \"');\n    s = s.replace(\n      /(\\bALTER\\s+TABLE\\s+\"[^\"]+\"\\.\"[^\"]+\"\\s+)ADD\\s+\"/i,\n      '$1ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS \"',\n    );\n    if (/^CREATE\\s+TYPE\\b/i.test(s)) {\n      s = `DO $$ BEGIN ${s}; EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL; END $$`;\n    }\n    return s;\n  };\n\n  describe('isValidatorRelevant — KEEP cases', () => {\n    it.each([\n      [\n        'CREATE TABLE for missing table',\n        `CREATE TABLE \"hr\".\"hr_outbox\" (\"id\" BIGSERIAL NOT NULL, \"eventType\" varchar(100))`,\n      ],\n      [\n        'CREATE TYPE for enum',\n        `CREATE TYPE \"hr\".\"shifts_shifttype_enum\" AS ENUM ('regular', 'overtime')`,\n      ],\n      [\n        'ALTER TABLE … ADD column (TypeORM omits COLUMN keyword)',\n        `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"payrolls\" ADD \"earningsBaseSalary\" numeric(12,2) NOT NULL`,\n      ],\n      [\n        'ALTER TABLE … ALTER COLUMN TYPE (uuid mismatch fix)',\n        `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"payrolls\" ALTER COLUMN \"tenantId\" TYPE uuid USING \"tenantId\"::uuid`,\n      ],\n      [\n        'ALTER TABLE … ALTER COLUMN SET NOT NULL',\n        `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"payrolls\" ALTER COLUMN \"tenantId\" SET NOT NULL`,\n      ],\n      [\n        'ALTER TABLE … ALTER COLUMN DROP DEFAULT (deploy 6 trio precondition)',\n        `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"training_enrollments\" ALTER COLUMN \"status\" DROP DEFAULT`,\n      ],\n      [\n        'ALTER TABLE … ALTER COLUMN SET DEFAULT (deploy 6 trio postcondition)',\n        `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"training_enrollments\" ALTER COLUMN \"status\" SET DEFAULT 'enrolled'`,\n      ],\n      [\n        'ALTER TABLE … ALTER COLUMN DROP NOT NULL (validator does not flag relaxed nullability)',\n        `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"weekly_plans\" ALTER COLUMN \"notifiedAt\" DROP NOT NULL`,\n      ],\n    ])('keeps %s', (_label, sql) => {\n      expect(isValidatorRelevant(sql)).toBe(true);\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('isValidatorRelevant — SKIP cases (validator does not check these)', () => {\n    it.each([\n      [\n        'ADD CONSTRAINT PRIMARY KEY (deploy 5 failure mode)',\n        `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"scheduling_settings\" ADD CONSTRAINT \"PK_8e3892ce17b3a09c6172d83423d\" PRIMARY KEY (\"tenantId\")`,\n      ],\n      [\n        'ADD CONSTRAINT UNIQUE',\n        `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"shifts\" ADD CONSTRAINT \"UQ_shift_tenant_code\" UNIQUE (\"tenantId\", \"code\")`,\n      ],\n      [\n        'ADD CONSTRAINT FOREIGN KEY',\n        `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"employees\" ADD CONSTRAINT \"FK_employee_department\" FOREIGN KEY (\"departmentId\") REFERENCES \"hr\".\"departments_hr\"(\"id\")`,\n      ],\n      [\n        'ADD CONSTRAINT CHECK',\n        `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"leave_requests\" ADD CONSTRAINT \"CK_dates\" CHECK (\"startDate\" <= \"endDate\")`,\n      ],\n      ['CREATE INDEX', `CREATE INDEX \"IDX_payroll_tenant\" ON \"hr\".\"payrolls\" (\"tenantId\")`],\n      ['DROP TABLE', `DROP TABLE \"hr\".\"old_table\"`],\n      [\n        'DROP TYPE (deploy 4 failure mode)',\n        `DROP TYPE \"hr\".\"work_week_day\"`,\n      ],\n      ['DROP COLUMN', `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"payrolls\" DROP COLUMN \"earnings\"`],\n      [\n        'DROP CONSTRAINT',\n        `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"employees\" DROP CONSTRAINT \"FK_old_dept\"`,\n      ],\n      ['COMMENT ON COLUMN', `COMMENT ON COLUMN \"hr\".\"payrolls\".\"tenantId\" IS 'tenant id'`],\n    ])('skips %s', (_label, sql) => {\n      expect(isValidatorRelevant(sql)).toBe(false);\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('makeIdempotent', () => {\n    it('rewrites CREATE TABLE → CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS', () => {\n      const input = `CREATE TABLE \"hr\".\"hr_outbox\" (\"id\" BIGSERIAL NOT NULL)`;\n      expect(makeIdempotent(input)).toBe(\n        `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS \"hr\".\"hr_outbox\" (\"id\" BIGSERIAL NOT NULL)`,\n      );\n    });\n\n    it('rewrites ALTER TABLE … ADD \"col\" → ALTER TABLE … ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS \"col\"', () => {\n      const input = `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"payrolls\" ADD \"earningsBaseSalary\" numeric(12,2) NOT NULL`;\n      expect(makeIdempotent(input)).toBe(\n        `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"payrolls\" ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS \"earningsBaseSalary\" numeric(12,2) NOT NULL`,\n      );\n    });\n\n    it('wraps CREATE TYPE in DO/EXCEPTION duplicate_object', () => {\n      const input = `CREATE TYPE \"hr\".\"shifts_shifttype_enum\" AS ENUM ('regular')`;\n      const out = makeIdempotent(input);\n      expect(out).toContain('DO $$ BEGIN');\n      expect(out).toContain(input);\n      expect(out).toContain('EXCEPTION WHEN duplicate_object THEN NULL');\n    });\n\n    it('does NOT touch ALTER COLUMN TYPE (no idempotent rewrite needed; PG is idempotent at type level)', () => {\n      const input = `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"payrolls\" ALTER COLUMN \"tenantId\" TYPE uuid USING \"tenantId\"::uuid`;\n      expect(makeIdempotent(input)).toBe(input);\n    });\n\n    it('does NOT touch ALTER COLUMN SET NOT NULL', () => {\n      const input = `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"payrolls\" ALTER COLUMN \"tenantId\" SET NOT NULL`;\n      expect(makeIdempotent(input)).toBe(input);\n    });\n\n    it('preserves schema-qualified table names through ADD COLUMN rewrite', () => {\n      const input = `ALTER TABLE \"tenant_abc123def456abc1\".\"payrolls\" ADD \"newCol\" varchar`;\n      expect(makeIdempotent(input)).toBe(\n        `ALTER TABLE \"tenant_abc123def456abc1\".\"payrolls\" ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS \"newCol\" varchar`,\n      );\n    });\n  });\n\n  describe('end-to-end pipeline (whitelist + idempotency)', () => {\n    it('keeps and rewrites a missing-column ADD scenario', () => {\n      const sql = `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"payrolls\" ADD \"earningsBaseSalary\" numeric(12,2) NOT NULL`;\n      expect(isValidatorRelevant(sql)).toBe(true);\n      expect(makeIdempotent(sql)).toBe(\n        `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"payrolls\" ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS \"earningsBaseSalary\" numeric(12,2) NOT NULL`,\n      );\n    });\n\n    it('rejects the deploy 5 PK conflict scenario before idempotency runs', () => {\n      const sql = `ALTER TABLE \"hr\".\"scheduling_settings\" ADD CONSTRAINT \"PK_x\" PRIMARY KEY (\"tenantId\")`;\n      expect(isValidatorRelevant(sql)).toBe(false);\n    });\n\n    it('rejects the deploy 4 DROP TYPE cascade scenario', () => {\n      const sql = `DROP TYPE \"hr\".\"work_week_day\"`;\n      expect(isValidatorRelevant(sql)).toBe(false);\n    });\n  });\n});\n","numLines":188,"startLine":1,"totalLines":188}}}

'../batch.handler';\n    test('handler', () => expect(new BatchHandler().execute()).toBe(true));\n  `,\n  'utf8',\n);\nwriteFileSync(\n  join(root, 'batch', 'unsafe.controller.ts'),\n  `\n    export class UnsafeController {\n      create() { return true; }\n    }\n  `,\n  'utf8',\n);\nwriteFileSync(\n  join(root, 'auth.guard.ts'),\n  `\n    export class AuthGuard {\n      canActivate() { return true; }\n    }\n  `,\n  'utf8',\n);\nwriteFileSync(\n  join(root, 'weak-only.guard.ts'),\n  `\n    export class WeakOnlyGuard {\n      canActivate() { return true; }\n    }\n  `,\n  'utf8',\n);\nwriteFileSync(\n  join(root, 'batch', '__tests__', 'weak-symbol.spec.ts'),\n  `\n    test('mentions symbol only', () => expect('WeakOnlyGuard').toBeTruthy());\n  `,\n  'utf8',\n);\nwriteFileSync(\n  join(root, 'database', 'migrations', '001-drop.ts'),\n  `\n    export class DropTable001 {\n      async up(queryRunner: any) {\n        await queryRunner.query('DROP TABLE bad');\n      }\n    }\n  `,\n  'utf8',\n);\n// E13 FP class (2): a hazardous migration retired into the `.archive/`\n// snapshot corpus is dead code — it must produce NO finding.\nmkdirSync(join(root, 'database', 'migrations', '.archive', '2026-01-01T00-00-00-000Z'), {\n  recursive: true,\n});\nwriteFileSync(\n  join(root, 'database', 'migrations', '.archive', '2026-01-01T00-00-00-000Z', '000-archived-drop.ts'),\n  `\n    export class ArchivedDrop000 {\n      async up(queryRunner: any) {\n        await queryRunner.query('DROP TABLE retired');\n      }\n    }\n  `,\n  'utf8',\n);\n// Deliberate-break trap: this spec basename-matches unsafe.controller.ts, but\n// it lives under an archived directory — if the archive filter ever stops\n// excluding it from the TEST corpus it would satisfy unsafe.controller's\n// coverage lookup and the finding asserted below would vanish.\nmkdirSync(join(root, 'batch', 'archive'), { recursive: true });\nwriteFileSync(\n  join(root, 'batch', 'archive', 'unsafe.controller.spec.ts'),\n  `\n    test('archived spec must not count as live coverage', () => expect(true).toBe(true));\n  `,\n  'utf8',\n);\n\nconst output = analyzeTestGaps({ roots: ['apps/farm-service/src'], includeWriteBoundaryFindings: true }, workspace);\n\nassert.equal(output.metadata.adapter, 'test-gap-adapter');\nassert.equal(output.observations.some((item) => item.type === 'test_gap_source_file'), true);\nassert.equal(output.observations.some((item) => item.type === 'test_gap_test_file'), true);\nassert.equal(output.observations.some((item) => item.type === 'test_gap_coverage_summary'), true);\nassert.equal(output.findings.some((finding) => finding.path.endsWith('batch.handler.ts')), false);\nassert.equal(\n  output.findings.some(\n    (finding) => finding.rule === 'high_risk_source_without_adjacent_test' && finding.path.endsWith('unsafe.controller.ts'),\n  ),\n  true,\n);\nassert.equal(\n  output.findings.some(\n    (finding) => finding.rule === 'security_source_without_security_test' && finding.path.endsWith('auth.guard.ts'),\n  ),\n  true,\n);\nassert.equal(\n  output.findings.some(\n    (finding) => finding.rule === 'security_source_without_security_test' && finding.path.endsWith('weak-only.guard.ts'),\n  ),\n  true,\n);\nassert.equal(\n  output.findings.some(\n    (finding) => finding.rule === 'migration_without_test' && finding.path.endsWith('001-drop.ts'),\n  ),\n  true,\n);\n// E13 FP class (2): archived corpus is fully excluded from the scan — no\n// findings against it, no observations for it, and it never appears in\n// read_paths.\nassert.equal(\n  output.findings.some((finding) => finding.path.includes('.archive')),\n  false,\n);\nassert.equal(\n  output.read_paths.some((path) => path.includes('.archive') || path.includes('/archive/')),\n  false,\n);\n\nprocess.stdout.write('test-gap-adapter tests passed\\n');\n","numLines":148,"startLine":1,"totalLines":148}}}

Test specs belong in a sibling\n6\t * `__tests__/` subdirectory — that is where the codebase-wide\n7\t * convention puts every other test, and where\n8\t * `apps/db-migrate/tsconfig.build.json` already excludes them.\n9\t *\n10\t * # Why this invariant exists\n11\t *\n12\t * `apps/db-migrate` is a single CLI that compiles every service's\n13\t * migration source tree into one bundle (see schema-registry.ts).\n14\t * Its `tsconfig.build.json` `include` block pulls each service's\n15\t * `migrations/` directory across `..` boundaries, so a bare\n16\t * `**\\/*.spec.ts` exclude pattern (anchored to db-migrate's tsconfig\n17\t * directory) does NOT reach the cross-tree spec files. The build\n18\t * scope discovers a spec, the spec uses Jest globals, and the\n19\t * production tsconfig deliberately omits `@types/jest` from `types`\n20\t * so that production bundles do not advertise Jest globals — the\n21\t * compile then fails with TS2593 / TS2304.\n22\t *\n23\t * The first instances of this regression were\n24\t * `apps/auth-service/src/migrations/*.spec.ts` and\n25\t * `apps/farm-service/src/database/migrations/*.spec.ts` (commit\n26\t * efddd723), which broke `build` and `Frontend Lighthouse CI` jobs\n27\t * on `main`. The fix moved the specs into `__tests__/` and added\n28\t * cross-tree exclude entries to `tsconfig.build.json` as a Tier-3\n29\t * safety net.\n30\t *\n31\t * This invariant locks in the Tier-1 shape of the fix: any future\n32\t * `*.spec.ts` placed directly inside a top-level `migrations/`\n33\t * directory of a service is a regression and is rejected at PR\n34\t * time, before it reaches the build scope.\n35\t *\n36\t * # Detection contract\n37\t *\n38\t * Walk every service under `apps/<svc>/src/`. For any directory whose\n39\t * leaf segment is `migrations` (i.e. `apps/<svc>/src/migrations/` or\n40\t * `apps/<svc>/src/database/migrations/`), assert that no immediate\n41\t * `.spec.ts` child file exists. Spec files inside a nested\n42\t * `__tests__/` are accepted — they are the canonical placement.\n43\t *\n44\t * # What this invariant does NOT check\n45\t *\n46\t *   - It does not enforce that test specs exist for every migration —\n47\t *     test coverage is a separate concern.\n48\t *   - It does not police `.test.ts` files (the same rule applies in\n49\t *     spirit; the regression only ever surfaced for `.spec.ts` and\n50\t *     `tsconfig.build.json` already covers `**\\/*.test.ts` per\n51\t *     migration tree as a belt-and-braces measure).\n52\t *   - It does not validate that `tsconfig.build.json`'s exclude\n53\t *     entries are paired with each include entry — that pairing is\n54\t *     enforced by the build itself: any future regression compiles\n55\t *     the spec into the bundle and trips TS2593, which `tsc -p\n56\t *     ...tsconfig.build.json` surfaces as a hard failure.\n57\t *\n58\t * # Closure\n59\t *\n60\t * Closes: orphan finding tracked by the build-failure plan at\n61\t * /tmp/ci-cleanup-plans/build-db-migrate.md §6 (\"Long-term invariant\").\n62\t */\n63\t\n64\timport { readdirSync, statSync } from 'node:fs';\n65\timport path from 'node:path';\n66\t\n67\tconst REPO_ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..', '..');\n68\tconst APPS_DIR = path.join(REPO_ROOT, 'apps');\n69\t\n70\t/**\n71\t * Recursively walk a directory and yield absolute paths whose leaf\n72\t * directory name is `migrations`. Stops descending into hidden or\n73\t * `node_modules` directories.\n74\t */\n75\tfunction findMigrationDirs(root: string): string[] {\n76\t  const out: string[] = [];\n77\t  const stack: string[] = [root];\n78\t  while (stack.length > 0) {\n79\t    const dir = stack.pop();\n80\t    if (!dir) continue;\n81\t    let entries: string[];\n82\t    try {\n83\t      entries = readdirSync(dir);\n84\t directories are not a\n101\t        // pattern in this codebase, and descending would re-flag\n102\t        // the same directory on its child path.\n103\t        continue;\n104\t      }\n105\t      stack.push(full);\n106\t    }\n107\t  }\n108\t  return out;\n109\t}\n110\t\n111\t/**\n112\t * List immediate `.spec.ts` children of a migrations/ directory.\n113\t * Files inside a nested `__tests__/` path.join(migrationsDir, e));\n134\t}\n135\t\n136\tdescribe('INVARIANT: migration spec quarantine', () => {\n137\t  it('asserts no .spec.ts file lives directly inside any service migrations/ Test specs belong in a sibling\n * `__tests__/` subdirectory — that is where the codebase-wide\n * convention puts every other test, and where\n * `apps/db-migrate/tsconfig.build.json` already excludes them.\n *\n * # Why this invariant exists\n *\n * `apps/db-migrate` is a single CLI that compiles every service's\n * migration source tree into one bundle (see schema-registry.ts).\n * Its `tsconfig.build.json` `include` block pulls each service's\n * `migrations/` directory across `..` boundaries, so a bare\n * `**\\/*.spec.ts` exclude pattern (anchored to db-migrate's tsconfig\n * directory) does NOT reach the cross-tree spec files. The build\n * scope discovers a spec, the spec uses Jest globals, and the\n * production tsconfig deliberately omits `@types/jest` from `types`\n * so that production bundles do not advertise Jest globals — the\n * compile then fails with TS2593 / TS2304.\n *\n * The first instances of this regression were\n * `apps/auth-service/src/migrations/*.spec.ts` and\n * `apps/farm-service/src/database/migrations/*.spec.ts` (commit\n * efddd723), which broke `build` and `Frontend Lighthouse CI` jobs\n * on `main`. The fix moved the specs into `__tests__/` and added\n * cross-tree exclude entries to `tsconfig.build.json` as a Tier-3\n * safety net.\n *\n * This invariant locks in the Tier-1 shape of the fix: any future\n * `*.spec.ts` placed directly inside a top-level `migrations/`\n * directory of a service is a regression and is rejected at PR\n * time, before it reaches the build scope.\n *\n * # Detection contract\n *\n * Walk every service under `apps/<svc>/src/`. For any directory whose\n * leaf segment is `migrations` (i.e. `apps/<svc>/src/migrations/` or\n * `apps/<svc>/src/database/migrations/`), assert that no immediate\n * `.spec.ts` child file exists. Spec files inside a nested\n * `__tests__/` are accepted — they are the canonical placement.\n *\n * # What this invariant does NOT check\n *\n *   - It does not enforce that test specs exist for every migration —\n *     test coverage is a separate concern.\n *   - It does not police `.test.ts` files (the same rule applies in\n *     spirit; the regression only ever surfaced for `.spec.ts` and\n *     `tsconfig.build.json` already covers `**\\/*.test.ts` per\n *     migration tree as a belt-and-braces measure).\n *   - It does not validate that `tsconfig.build.json`'s exclude\n *     entries are paired with each include entry — that pairing is\n *     enforced by the build itself: any future regression compiles\n *     the spec into the bundle and trips TS2593, which `tsc -p\n *     ...tsconfig.build.json` surfaces as a hard failure.\n *\n * # Closure\n *\n * Closes: orphan finding tracked by the build-failure plan at\n * /tmp/ci-cleanup-plans/build-db-migrate.md §6 (\"Long-term invariant\").\n */\n\nimport { readdirSync, statSync } from 'node:fs';\nimport path from 'node:path';\n\nconst REPO_ROOT = path.resolve(__dirname, '..', '..');\nconst APPS_DIR = path.join(REPO_ROOT, 'apps');\n\n/**\n * Recursively walk a directory and yield absolute paths whose leaf\n * directory name is `migrations`. Stops descending into hidden or\n * `node_modules` directories.\n */\nfunction findMigrationDirs(root: string): string[] {\n  const out: string[] = [];\n  const stack: string[] = [root];\n  while (stack.length > 0) {\n    const dir = stack.pop();\n    if (!dir) continue;\n    let entries: string[];\n    try {\n      entries = readdirSync(dir);\n    } catch {\n      continue;\n    }\n    for (const entry of entries) {\n      if (entry.startsWith('.') || entry === 'node_modules') continue;\n      const full = path.join(dir, entry);\n      let st: ReturnType<typeof statSync>;\n      try {\n        st = statSync(full);\n      } catch {\n        continue;\n      }\n      if (!st.isDirectory()) continue;\n      if (entry === 'migrations') {\n        out.push(full);\n        // Do NOT descend into `migrations/` itself for further\n        // discovery — nested `migrations/` directories are not a\n        // pattern in this codebase, and descending would re-flag\n        // the same directory on its child path.\n        continue;\n      }\n      stack.push(full);\n    }\n  }\n  return out;\n}\n\n/**\n * List immediate `.spec.ts` children of a migrations/ directory.\n * Files inside a nested `__tests__/` are NOT immediate children\n * and are not returned.\n */\nfunction listTopLevelSpecs(migrationsDir: string): string[] {\n  let entries: string[];\n  try {\n    entries = readdirSync(migrationsDir);\n  } catch {\n    return [];\n  }\n  return entries\n    .filter((e) => e.endsWith('.spec.ts'))\n    .filter((e) => {\n      const full = path.join(migrationsDir, e);\n      try {\n        return statSync(full).isFile();\n      } catch {\n        return false;\n      }\n    })\n    .map((e) => path.join(migrationsDir, e));\n}\n\ndescribe('INVARIANT: migration spec quarantine', () => {\n  it('asserts no .spec.ts file lives directly inside any service migrations/ directory', () => {\n    let services: string[];\n    try {\n      services = readdirSync(APPS_DIR);\n    } catch (err) {\n      throw new Error(\n        `apps/ directory not readable at ${APPS_DIR}: ${(err as Error).message}`,\n      );\n    }\n\n    const violations: string[] = [];\n    for (const svc of services) {\n      const svcSrc = path.join(APPS_DIR, svc, 'src');\n      let st: ReturnType<typeof statSync>;\n      try {\n        st = statSync(svcSrc);\n      } catch {\n        continue; // service has no src/ — not applicable\n      }\n      if (!st.isDirectory()) continue;\n\n      const migrationDirs = findMigrationDirs(svcSrc);\n      for (const dir of migrationDirs) {\n        const specs = listTopLevelSpecs(dir);\n        for (const spec of specs) {\n          violations.push(path.relative(REPO_ROOT, spec));\n        }\n      }\n    }\n\n    if (violations.length > 0) {\n      const lines = violations.map((v) => `  - ${v}`).join('\\n');\n      throw new Error(\n        [\n          'Found .spec.ts file(s) placed directly inside a service migrations/ directory.',\n          'These break `db-migrate:build:production` because the db-migrate tsconfig',\n          'pulls every service migrations/ tree across `..` boundaries while keeping',\n          '`@types/jest` deliberately out of `types` (production bundles must not',\n          'advertise Jest globals). Move each spec into a sibling `__tests__/`',\n          'subdirectory:',\n          '',\n          lines,\n          '',\n          'Example:',\n          '  apps/<svc>/src/migrations/2026-foo.spec.ts',\n          '   -> apps/<svc>/src/migrations/__tests__/2026-foo.spec.ts',\n          '',\n          'See /tmp/ci-cleanup-plans/build-db-migrate.md for the full rationale,',\n          'and `apps/db-migrate/tsconfig.build.json` for the matching exclude',\n          'entries that act as the Tier-3 safety net.',\n        ].join('\\n'),\n      );\n    }\n  });\n});\n","numLines":192,"startLine":1,"totalLines":192}}}

Names are\n111\t * not unique across schemas, so a per-tenant table whose name also exists in a\n112\t * platform schema reads as a trust-boundary violation when it is nothing of the\n113\t * kind: `messages` lives in `auth` and `admin` AND is messaging's per-tenant\n114\t * table; `retention_policies` and `legal_holds` collide the same way.\n115\t *\n116\t * That never surfaced because B.5b had never run against a database with a real\n117\t * tenant in it — B.5a was passing vacuously against zero tenants for the same\n118\t * reason. The first genuinely provisioned tenant produced four false positives\n119\t * and no true ones.\n120\t *\n121\t * Subtracting the registered per-tenant names keeps the assertion pointed at\n122\t * what it means: a tenant table that is a clone of something only a platform\n123\t * schema owns. A platform table that ALSO gets registered per-tenant would stop\n124\t * being caught here — but that registration is itself the violation, and\n125\t * `entity-schema-declaration` and B.1/B.2 are the gates that own it.\n126\t */\n127\tconst REGISTERED_TENANT_TABLE_NAMES: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set(\n128\t  MODULE_SCHEMAS.flatMap((moduleSchema) => [\n129\t    ...moduleSchema.tables,\n130\t    ...(moduleSchema.referenceDataTables ?? []),\n131\t  ]),\n132\t);\n133\t\n134\t// The TENANT_SCOPED set is also the allow-list for entities legitimately\n135\t// declaring `schema: undefined` (farm-pattern: SchemaManagerService\n136\t// routes them at provision time, so the @Entity decorator stays\n137\t// schema-agnostic). Any entity outside this allow-list that omits\n138\t// `schema:` is a regression — see assertion B.1.\n139\tconst TENANT_SCOPED_SERVICES: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set([\n140\t  'farm-service',\n141\t  'sensor-service',\n142\t  'hr-service',\n143\t  'messaging-service',\n144\t  'hydroponics-service',\n145\t  'ai-service',\n146\t  'alert-engine',\n147\t]);\n148\t\n149\t/**"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"9842d2e2-2674-4c40-84ec-bcd1c4474f20","uuid":"31ba13aa-26b3-4fbc-a92a-7c46930d1f3e","timestamp":"2026-09-19T16:27:08.205Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-a29dcc8b0754/e2e/tests/integration/schema-invariants.spec.ts","content":"  'event_store',\n  'observability',\n  'config',\n  'gateway',\n  'shared',\n  // Registry-completeness sweep (ORPHAN-HIGH-365): compliance (legal_holds,\n  // admin-api-owned) + platform (db-migrate bootstrap tables) — single\n  // cross-tenant schemas, never per-tenant cloned.\n  'compliance',\n  'platform',\n];\n\nconst TENANT_FANOUT_TABLES_BY_SCHEMA: ReadonlyMap<string, ReadonlyArray<string>> = new Map(\n  MODULE_SCHEMAS.map((moduleSchema) => [moduleSchema.sourceSchema, moduleSchema.tables]),\n);\n\n/**\n * Every table name a tenant schema is SUPPOSED to carry, from the registry.\n *\n * B.5b looks for platform-schema tables cloned into a tenant, and it can only\n * compare by NAME — `information_schema` has no notion of \"clone of\". Names are\n * not unique across schemas, so a per-tenant table whose name also exists in a\n * platform schema reads as a trust-boundary violation when it is nothing of the\n * kind: `messages` lives in `auth` and `admin` AND is messaging's per-tenant\n * table; `retention_policies` and `legal_holds` collide the same way.\n *\n * That never surfaced because B.5b had never run against a database with a real\n * tenant in it — B.5a was passing vacuously against zero tenants for the same\n * reason. The first genuinely provisioned tenant produced four false positives\n * and no true ones.\n *\n * Subtracting the registered per-tenant names keeps the assertion pointed at\n * what it means: a tenant table that is a clone of something only a platform\n * schema owns. A platform table that ALSO gets registered per-tenant would stop\n * being caught here — but that registration is itself the violation, and\n * `entity-schema-declaration` and B.1/B.2 are the gates that own it.\n */\nconst REGISTERED_TENANT_TABLE_NAMES: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set(\n  MODULE_SCHEMAS.flatMap((moduleSchema) => [\n    ...moduleSchema.tables,\n    ...(moduleSchema.referenceDataTables ?? []),\n  ]),\n);\n\n// The TENANT_SCOPED set is also the allow-list for entities legitimately\n// declaring `schema: undefined` (farm-pattern: SchemaManagerService\n// routes them at provision time, so the @Entity decorator stays\n// schema-agnostic). Any entity outside this allow-list that omits\n// `schema:` is a regression — see assertion B.1.\nconst TENANT_SCOPED_SERVICES: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set([\n  'farm-service',\n  'sensor-service',\n  'hr-service',\n  'messaging-service',\n  'hydroponics-service',\n  'ai-service',\n  'alert-engine',\n]);\n\n/**","numLines":60,"startLine":90,"totalLines":746}}}

Part C\n672\t        // spec actively provisions a tenant and runs the same check\n673\t        // with stronger preconditions.\n674\t        return;\n675\t      }\n676\t      // Sample one fan-out table from the SchemaManagerService SSoT.\n677\t      // Source schemas may also contain source-only infrastructure\n678\t      // tables (migrations, outbox, audit ledgers) that must NOT be\n679\t      // tenant-cloned.\n680\t      const sourceTables = TENANT_FANOUT_TABLES_BY_SCHEMA.get(sourceSchema) ?? [];\n681\t      const sampleTable = [...sourceTables].sort()[0];\n682\t      if (!sampleTable) {\n683\t        // No fan-out tables declared — nothing to clone, nothing to\n684\t        // assert. MODULE_SCHEMAS static invariants cover declaration\n685\t        // completeness.\n686\t        return;\n687\t      }\n688\t      const cloneCheck = await db.query<{ count: string }>(\n689\t        `SELECT COUNT(*)::text AS count FROM information_schema.tables\n690\t         WHERE table_schema ~ '^tenant_[a-f0-9]{16}$'\n691\t           AND table_name = $1`,\n692\t        [sampleTable],\n693\t      );\n694\t      const cloneCount = parseInt(cloneCheck.rows[0]?.count ?? '0', 10);\n695\t      if (cloneCount === 0) {\n696\t        throw new Error(\n697\t          `B.5a tenant-scoped schema \"${sourceSchema}\" has source table ` +\n698\t            `\"${sampleTable}\" but ZERO tenant_<uuid> schemas carry a clone of it. ` +\n699\t            `TenantSchemaSyncService is not provisioning the table on tenant ` +\n700\t            `creation, or every existing tenant predates the table's introduction ` +\n701\t            `(in which case run the deploy-time tenant fan-out).`,\n702\t        );\n703\t      }\n704\t    },\n705\t  );\n706\t\n707\t  // B.5b — PLATFORM_LEVEL schemas: each MUST have ZERO tenant_<uuid>\n708\t  // clones of its tables. A tenant clone of e.g. `auth.users` is a\n709\t  // tenant-isolation hole — auth is the cross-tenant trust anchor.\n710\t  it.each(PLATFORM_LEVEL_SCHEMAS)(\n711\t    'B.5b — platform-level schema \"%s\" has ZERO tenant_<uuid> clones of its tables',\n712\t    async (platformSchema) => {\n713\t      const sourceTables = await db.query<{ table_name: string }>(\n714\t        `SELECT table_name FROM information_schema.tables\n715\t         WHERE table_schema = $1 AND table_type = 'BASE TABLE'`,\n716\t        [platformSchema],\n717\t      );\n718\t      const sourceTableNames = sourceTables.rows\n719\t        .map((r) => r.table_name)"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"9842d2e2-2674-4c40-84ec-bcd1c4474f20","uuid":"a8bd45ad-f1f1-45e9-ab78-86fe3ae89ce8","timestamp":"2026-09-19T16:27:09.330Z","tool_use_result":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/home/gharunner/actions-runner/_work/aquaculture_platform/aquaculture_platform/aria-worktrees/req-AIR-aria-evidence-judge-a29dcc8b0754/e2e/tests/integration/schema-invariants.spec.ts","content":"  it('B.4b — schema-owner roles are NOLOGIN', async () => {\n    const owners = LEAST_PRIVILEGE_SPECS.map((s) => s.owner_role);\n    const result = await db.query<{ rolname: string }>(\n      `SELECT rolname FROM pg_catalog.pg_roles\n        WHERE rolname = ANY($1::text[]) AND rolcanlogin`,\n      [owners],\n    );\n    const offenders = result.rows.map(\n      (row) =>\n        `${row.rolname} can log in — a schema-owner role is DDL authority and must be ` +\n        `reachable only through GRANT <owner> TO db_migrate, never as a credential`,\n    );\n    expect(offenders).toEqual([]);\n  });\n\n  // B.5a — TENANT_SCOPED schemas: each MUST have at least one\n  // tenant_<uuid> clone of its source tables in the test DB.\n  // Runs only when the test DB has at least one provisioned tenant\n  // (the farm-service migration baseline + e2e fixtures provision a\n  // smoke tenant). If no tenant exists, the assertion is vacuously\n  // satisfied — the bootstrap path doesn't provision tenants by\n  // itself.\n  it.each(TENANT_SCOPED_SCHEMAS)(\n    'B.5a — tenant-scoped schema \"%s\" has at least one tenant clone (when tenants exist)',\n    async (sourceSchema) => {\n      // Inventory of tenant clone schemas in the DB.\n      const tenantSchemas = await db.query<{ schema_name: string }>(\n        `SELECT schema_name FROM information_schema.schemata\n         WHERE schema_name ~ '^tenant_[a-f0-9]{16}$'`,\n      );\n      if (tenantSchemas.rows.length === 0) {\n        // No tenants provisioned — assertion vacuously true. Part C\n        // spec actively provisions a tenant and runs the same check\n        // with stronger preconditions.\n        return;\n      }\n      // Sample one fan-out table from the SchemaManagerService SSoT.\n      // Source schemas may also contain source-only infrastructure\n      // tables (migrations, outbox, audit ledgers) that must NOT be\n      // tenant-cloned.\n      const sourceTables = TENANT_FANOUT_TABLES_BY_SCHEMA.get(sourceSchema) ?? [];\n      const sampleTable = [...sourceTables].sort()[0];\n      if (!sampleTable) {\n        // No fan-out tables declared — nothing to clone, nothing to\n        // assert. MODULE_SCHEMAS static invariants cover declaration\n        // completeness.\n        return;\n      }\n      const cloneCheck = await db.query<{ count: string }>(\n        `SELECT COUNT(*)::text AS count FROM information_schema.tables\n         WHERE table_schema ~ '^tenant_[a-f0-9]{16}$'\n           AND table_name = $1`,\n        [sampleTable],\n      );\n      const cloneCount = parseInt(cloneCheck.rows[0]?.count ?? '0', 10);\n      if (cloneCount === 0) {\n        throw new Error(\n          `B.5a tenant-scoped schema \"${sourceSchema}\" has source table ` +\n            `\"${sampleTable}\" but ZERO tenant_<uuid> schemas carry a clone of it. ` +\n            `TenantSchemaSyncService is not provisioning the table on tenant ` +\n            `creation, or every existing tenant predates the table's introduction ` +\n            `(in which case run the deploy-time tenant fan-out).`,\n        );\n      }\n    },\n  );\n\n  // B.5b — PLATFORM_LEVEL schemas: each MUST have ZERO tenant_<uuid>\n  // clones of its tables. A tenant clone of e.g. `auth.users` is a\n  // tenant-isolation hole — auth is the cross-tenant trust anchor.\n  it.each(PLATFORM_LEVEL_SCHEMAS)(\n    'B.5b — platform-level schema \"%s\" has ZERO tenant_<uuid> clones of its tables',\n    async (platformSchema) => {\n      const sourceTables = await db.query<{ table_name: string }>(\n        `SELECT table_name FROM information_schema.tables\n         WHERE table_schema = $1 AND table_type = 'BASE TABLE'`,\n        [platformSchema],\n      );\n      const sourceTableNames = sourceTables.rows\n        .map((r) => r.table_name)","numLines":80,"startLine":640,"totalLines":746}}}
